{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /etc/claude-code/.claude/skills/manufacturing-failure-reason-codebook-normalization\n\nThis skill should be considered when you need to normalize, standardize, or correct testing engineers' written failure reasons to match the requirements provided in the product codebooks. Common errors in engineer-written reasons include ambiguous descriptions, missing important words, improper personal writing habits, using wrong abbreviations, improper combining multiple reasons into one sentence without clear spacing or in wrong order, writing wrong station names or model, writing typos, improper combining Chinese and English characters, cross-project differences, and taking wrong products' codebook.\n\nSome codes are defined for specific stations and cannot be used by other stations. If entry.stations is not None, the predicted code should only be considered valid when the record station matches one of the stations listed in entry.stations. Otherwise, the code should be rejected. For each record segment, the system evaluates candidate codes defined in the corresponding product codebook and computes an internal matching score for each candidate. You should consider multiple evidence sources to calculate the score to measure how well a candidate code explains the segment, and normalize the score to a stable range [0.0, 1.0]. Evidence can include text evidence from raw_reason_text (e.g., overlap or fuzzy similarity between span_text and codebook text such as standard_label, keywords_examples, or categories), station compatibility, fail_code alignment, test_item alignment, and conflict cues such as mutually exclusive or contradictory signals. After all candidate codes are scored, sort them in descending order. Let c1 be the top candidate with score s1 and c2 be the second candidate with score s2. When multiple candidates fall within a small margin of the best score, the system applies a deterministic tie-break based on record context (e.g., record_id, segment index, station, fail_code, test_item) to avoid always choosing the same code in near-tie cases while keeping outputs reproducible. To provide convincing answers, add station, fail_code, test_item, a short token overlap cue, or a component reference to the rationale.\n\nUNKNOWN handling: UNKNOWN should be decided based on the best match only (i.e., after ranking), not by marking multiple candidates. If the best-match score is low (weak evidence), output pred_code=\"UNKNOWN\" and pred_label=\"\" to give engineering an alert. When strong positive cues exist (e.g., clear component references), UNKNOWN should be less frequent than in generic or noisy segments.\n\nConfidence calibration: confidence ranges from 0.0 to 1.0 and reflects an engineering confidence level (not a probability). Calibrate confidence from match quality so that UNKNOWN predictions are generally less confident than non-UNKNOWN predictions, and confidence values are not nearly constant. Confidence should show distribution-level separation between UNKNOWN and non-UNKNOWN predictions (e.g., means, quantiles, and diversity), and should be weakly aligned with evidence strength; round confidence to 4 decimals.\n\nHere is a pipeline reference\n1) Load test_center_logs.csv into logs_rows and load each product codebook; build valid_code_set, station_scope_map, and CodebookEntry objects.  \n2) For each record, split raw_reason_text into 1–N segments; each segment uses segment_id=<record_id>-S<i> and keeps an exact substring as span_text.  \n3) For each segment, filter candidates by station scope, then compute match score from combined evidence (text evidence, station compatibility, context alignment, and conflict cues).  \n4) Rank candidates by score; if multiple are within a small margin of the best, choose deterministically using a context-dependent tie-break among near-best station-compatible candidates.  \n5) Output exactly one pred_code/pred_label per segment from the product codebook (or UNKNOWN/\"\" when best evidence is weak) and compute confidence by calibrating match quality with sufficient diversity; round to 4 decimals.\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f4d3f3c7-044d-4bd3-b4e3-2cdb2a654317","uuid":"1581e9d6-1b27-4e06-b3b8-8c4b41dd5155","isSynthetic":true}

re.sub(r'\\bOS\\b', 'open', text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n    text = re.sub(r'虚汉', '虚焊', text)  # Common typo\n    text = re.sub(r'接處', '接触', text)  # Common typo\n    text = re.sub(r'短露', '短路', text)  # Common typo\n    text = re.sub(r'治俱', '治具', text)  # Common typo\n    return text\n\n# Segmentation patterns\nSEGMENT_DELIMITERS = [\n    r'\\s*/\\s*',  # / delimiter\n    r'\\s*;\\s*',  # ; Tuple[float, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Score a candidate code against a text segment.\n    Returns (score, evidence_dict).\n    \"\"\"\n    evidence = {\n        'keyword_overlap': 0,\n = normalize_text(text)\n    matches = 0\n    total = len(entry.keywords) if entry.keywords else 1\n\n    for kw in entry.keywords:\n        if kw.lower() in norm_text:\n            matches += 1\n\n    return matches / total if total > 0 else 0\n\ndef compute_label_similarity(text: str, entry: CodebookEntry) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Compute similarity with standard label.\"\"\"\n    norm_text = normalize_text(text)\n    norm_label = normalize_text(entry.standard_label)\n\n    # Check for key terms in label\n    label_words = set(re.findall(r'\\w+', norm_label))\n    text_words = set(re.findall(r'\\w+', norm_text))\n\n    if not label_words:\n        return 0\n\n    overlap = len(label_words & text_words)\n    return overlap / len(label_words)\n\ndef compute_category_match(text: str, entry: CodebookEntry) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Check if text contains category-related terms.\"\"\"\n    norm_text = normalize_text(text)\n    score = 0\n\n    category_terms = {\n        'OPEN': ['open', '开路', 'os', '像开路'],\n        'SHORT': ['short', '短路', 'bridge', '桥连', '连锡'],\n        'COLD_SOLDER': ['cold solder', '虚焊', '浮高', 'intermittent', '不稳', '功能不稳'],\n        'FIXTURE_CONTACT': ['fixture', 'pogo', '接触不良', '探针', 'reseat', '治具', '重插'],\n        'FLASH_FAIL': ['flash', '烧录失败', 'write fail', 'program fail', '写入失败'],\n        'LEAKAGE': Tuple[float, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Score a candidate code against a text segment.\n    Returns (score, evidence_dict).\n    \"\"\"\n    evidence = {\n        'keyword_overlap': 0,\n        'label_similarity': 0,\n        'category_match': 0,\n        'component_match': 0,\n        'station_compat': 0,\n        'context_align': 0,\n    }\n\n    # 1. Keyword overlap (weight: 0.25)\n    evidence['keyword_overlap'] = compute_keyword_overlap(text, entry)\n\n    # 2. Label similarity (weight: 0.2)\n    evidence['label_similarity'] = compute_label_similarity(text, entry)\n\n    # 3. Category match (weight: 0.25)\n    evidence['category_match'] = compute_category_match(text, entry)\n\n    # 4. Component match (weight: 0.15)\n    text_comp = extract_component_ref(text)\n    label_comp = extract_component_ref(entry.standard_label)\n    if text_comp and label_comp and text_comp == label_comp:\n        evidence['component_match'] = 1.0\n    elif text_comp and text_comp in entry.standard_label.upper():\n        evidence['component_match'] = 0.8\n\n    # 5. Station compatibility (weight: 0.1)\n    record_station = record.get('station', '')\n    if not entry.station_scope or record_station in entry.station_scope:\n        evidence['station_compat'] = 1.0\n    else:\n        evidence['station_compat'] = 0.0\n\n    # 6. Context alignment - test_item, fail_code (weight: 0.05)\n    test_item = record.get('test_item', '')\n    if test_item:\n        net_in_text = extract_net_ref(text)\n        if net_in_text and net_in_text in entry.standard_label.upper():\n            evidence['context_align'] = 0.5\n        if test_item.upper() in entry.standard_label.upper():\n            evidence['context_align'] = 1.0\n\n    # Compute weighted score\n    weights = {\n        'keyword_overlap': 0.25,\n        'label_similarity': 0.2,\n        'category_match': 0.25,\n        'component_match': 0.15,\n        'station_compat': 0.1,\n        'context_align': 0.05,\n    }\n\n    total_score = sum(evidence[k] * weights[k] for k in weights)\n\n    return total_score, evidence\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(candidates: List[Tuple[float, CodebookEntry, Dict]],\n                           record: Dict, segment_idx: int) -> Tuple[CodebookEntry, float, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Deterministic tie-break among near-best candidates.\n    Uses record context to choose among candidates within margin.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not candidates:\n        return None, 0, {}\n\n    # Sort by score descending\n    candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)\n\n    best_score = candidates[0][0]\n    margin = 0.05  # Consider candidates within 5% of best\n\n    # Get near-best candidates\n    near_best = [c for c in candidates if c[0] >= best_score - margin]\n\n    if len(near_best) == 1:\n        return near_best[0][1], near_best[0][0], near_best[0][2]\n\n    # Deterministic tie-break using record context\n    # Create a hash based on record_id, segment_idx, station, fail_code, test_item\n    context_str = f\"{record['record_id']}-{segment_idx}-{record.get('station','')}-{record.get('fail_code','')}-{record.get('test_item','')}\"\n    context_hash = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n\n    # UNKNOWN: confidence range [0.15, 0.45]\n        base = 0.15 + score * 0.3\n        # Add some variation based on evidence\n        variation = evidence.get('keyword_overlap', 0) * 0.05\n        confidence = min(base + variation, 0.45)\n    else:\n        # Non-UNKNOWN: confidence range [0.5, 0.95]\n        base = 0.5 + score * 0.45\n        # Boost for strong evidence\n        if evidence.get('component_match', 0) > 0.5:\n            base += 0.05\n        if evidence.get('category_match', 0) > 0.5:\n            base += 0.05\n        confidence = min(base, 0.95)\n\n    return round(confidence, 4)\n\ndef generate_rationale(entry: Optional[CodebookEntry], evidence: Dict, record: Dict,\n                       is_unknown: bool, span_text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Generate rationale string for the prediction.\"\"\"\n    parts = []\n\n    if is_unknown:\n        parts.append(\"Low match confidence\")\n        if evidence.get('keyword_overlap', 0) < 0.2:\n            parts.append(\"weak keyword overlap\")\n        parts.append(f\"station={record.get('station','')}\")\n        return \"; \".join(parts)\n\n    #  # Weight longer keywords more\n            if len(kw_lower) >= 4:\n                weighted_matches += 2\n            else:\n                weighted_matches += 1\n\n    total = len(entry.keywords) score_candidate(text: str, entry: CodebookEntry, record: Dict, hint: Optional[str]) -> Tuple[float, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Score a candidate code against a text segment.\n    Returns (score, evidence_dict).\n    \"\"\"\n    evidence = {\n        'keyword_score': 0,\n        'category_score': 0,\n        'component_match': 0,\n        'net_match': 0,\n        'station_compat': 1.0,\n        'test_item_align': 0,\n    }\n\n    norm_text = normalize_text(text)\n\n    # 1. Keyword score (weight: 0.3)\n    evidence['keyword_score'] = compute_keyword_score(text, entry)\n\n    # 2. Category score (weight: 0.3)\n    evidence['category_score'] = compute_category_score(text, entry, hint)\n\n    # 3. Component match (weight: 0.15)\n    text_comp = extract_component_ref(text)\n    label_comp = extract_component_ref(entry.standard_label)\n    if text_comp and label_comp:\n        if text_comp == label_comp:\n            evidence['component_match'] = 1.0\n        elif text_comp[0] == label_comp[0]:  # Same component type\n            evidence['component_match'] = 0.3\n    elif text_comp and text_comp in entry.standard_label.upper():\n        evidence['component_match'] = 0.8\n\n    # 4. Net match (weight: 0.1)\n    text_net = extract_net_ref(text)\n    if text_net and text_net in entry.standard_label.upper():\n        evidence['net_match'] = 1.0\n\n    # 5. Station compatibility (weight: 0.1)\n    record_station = record.get('station', '')\n    if entry.station_scope and record_station not in entry.station_scope:\n        evidence['station_compat'] = 0.0\n\n    # 6. Test item alignment (weight: 0.05)\n    test_item = record.get('test_item', '')\n    if test_item:\n        if test_item.upper() in entry.standard_label.upper():\n            evidence['test_item_align'] = 1.0\n        elif extract_net_ref(test_item) and extract_net_ref(test_item) in entry.standard_label.upper():\n            evidence['test_item_align'] = 0.5\n\n    # Compute weighted score\n    weights = {\n        'keyword_score': 0.30,\n        'category_score': 0.30,\n        'component_match': 0.15,\n        'net_match': 0.10,\n        'station_compat': 0.10,\n        'test_item_align': 0.05,\n    }\n\n    total_score = sum(evidence[k] * weights[k] for k in weights)\n\n    return total_score, evidence\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(candidates: List[Tuple[float, CodebookEntry, Dict]],\n                           record: Dict, segment_idx: int) -> Tuple[Optional[CodebookEntry], float, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Deterministic tie-break among near-best candidates.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not candidates:\n        return None, 0, {}\n\n    candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)\n    best_score = candidates[0][0]\n    margin = 0.05\n\n    near_best = [c for c in candidates if c[0] >= best_score - margin]\n\n    if len(near_best) == 1:\n        return near_best[0][1], near_best[0][0], near_best[0][2]\n\n    # Deterministic tie-break\n    context_str = f\"{record['record_id']}-{segment_idx}-{record.get('station','')}-{record.get('fail_code','')}-{record.get('test_item','')}\"\n    context_hash = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n    selected_idx = context_hash % len(near_best)\n    selected = near_best[selected_idx]\n\n    return selected[1], selected[0], selected[2]\n\ndef calibrate_confidence(score: float, is_unknown: bool, evidence: Dict, record: Dict) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calibrate confidence from match score with sufficient diversity.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Use record context to add variation\n    context_str = f\"{record['record_id']}-{record.get('station','')}-{record.get('fail_code','')}\"\n    context_hash = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n    variation = (context_hash % 1000) / 10000  # 0 to 0.1\n\n    if is_unknown:\n        # UNKNOWN: confidence range [0.10, 0.48]\n        base = 0.10 + score * 0.35\n        # Add variation based on evidence quality\n        if evidence.get('keyword_score', 0) > 0.1:\n            base += 0.03\n        if evidence.get('category_score', 0) > 0.1:\n            base += 0.02\n        confidence = base + variation * 0.3\n        confidence = max(0.10, min(0.48, confidence))\n    else:\n        # Non-UNKNOWN: confidence range [0.52, 0.98]\n        base = 0.52 + score * 0.40\n        # Boost for strong evidence\n        if evidence.get('component_match', 0) > 0.5:\n            base += 0.03\n        if evidence.get('category_score', 0) > 308→        'net_match': 0.10,\n   309→        'station_compat': 0.10,\n   310→        'test_item_align': 0.05,\n   311→    }\n   312→\n   313→    total_score = sum(evidence[k] * weights[k] for k in Dict, segment_idx: int) -> Tuple[Optional[CodebookEntry], float, Dict]:\n   319→    \"\"\"\n   320→    Deterministic tie-break among near-best candidates.\n   321→    \"\"\"\n   322→    if not 329→    near_best = [c for c in candidates if c[0] >= best_score - margin]\n   330→\n   331→    if len(near_best) == 1:\n   332→        return near_best[0][1], near_best[0][0], near_best[0][2]\n   333→\n   334→    # Deterministic tie-break\n   335→    context_str = f\"{record['record_id']}-{segment_idx}-{record.get('station','')}-{record.get('fail_code','')}-{record.get('test_item','')}\"\n   336→    context_hash = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n   337→    selected_idx = context_hash % len(near_best)\n   338→    selected = near_best[selected_idx]\n   339→\n   340→        base += 0.02\n   359→        confidence = base + variation * 0.3\n   360→        confidence = max(0.10, min(0.48, confidence))\n   361→    else:\n   362→        # Non-UNKNOWN: confidence range 367→        if evidence.get('category_score', 0) > 0.5:\n   368→            base += 0.02\n   369→        if evidence.get('keyword_score', 0) > 0.5:\n   370→            base += 0.03\n   371→ contains category-related terms.\"\"\"\n    norm_text = normalize_text(text)\n    score = 0\n\n    cat = entry.category_lv2\n\n    # Bonus if hint matches\n    if hint and hint == cat:\n        score score_candidate(text: str, entry: CodebookEntry, record: Dict, hint: Optional[str]) -> Tuple[float, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Score a candidate code against a text segment.\n    Returns (score, evidence_dict).\n    \"\"\"\n    evidence = {\n        'keyword_score': 0,\n        'category_score': 0,\n        'component_match': 0,\n        'net_match': 0,\n        'station_compat': 1.0,\n        'test_item_align': 0,\n    }\n\n    norm_text = normalize_text(text)\n\n    # 1. Keyword score (weight: 0.3)\n    evidence['keyword_score'] = compute_keyword_score(text, entry)\n\n    # 2. Category score (weight: 0.3)\n    evidence['category_score'] = compute_category_score(text, entry, hint)\n\n    # 3. Component match (weight: 0.15)\n    text_comp = extract_component_ref(text)\n    label_comp = extract_component_ref(entry.standard_label)\n    if text_comp and label_comp:\n        if text_comp == label_comp:\n            evidence['component_match'] = 1.0\n        elif text_comp[0] == label_comp[0]:  # Same component type\n            evidence['component_match'] = 0.3\n    elif text_comp and text_comp in entry.standard_label.upper():\n        evidence['component_match'] = 0.8\n\n    # 4. Net match (weight: 0.1)\n    text_net = extract_net_ref(text)\n    if text_net and text_net in entry.standard_label.upper():\n        evidence['net_match'] = 1.0\n\n    # 5. Station compatibility (weight: 0.1)\n    record_station = record.get('station', '')\n    if entry.station_scope and record_station not in entry.station_scope:\n        evidence['station_compat'] = 0.0\n\n    # 6. Test item alignment (weight: 0.05)\n    test_item = record.get('test_item', '')\n    if test_item:\n        if test_item.upper() in entry.standard_label.upper():\n            evidence['test_item_align'] = 1.0\n        elif extract_net_ref(test_item) and extract_net_ref(test_item) in entry.standard_label.upper():\n            evidence['test_item_align'] = 0.5\n\n    # Compute weighted score\n    weights = {\n        'keyword_score': 0.30,\n        'category_score': 0.30,\n        'component_match': 0.15,\n        'net_match': 0.10,\n        'station_compat': 0.10,\n        'test_item_align': 0.05,\n    }\n\n    total_score = sum(evidence[k] * weights[k] for k in weights)\n\n    return total_score, evidence\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(candidates: List[Tuple[float, CodebookEntry, Dict]],\n                           record: Dict, segment_idx: int) -> Tuple[Optional[CodebookEntry], float, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Deterministic tie-break among near-best candidates.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not candidates:\n        return None, 0, {}\n\n    candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)\n    best_score = candidates[0][0]\n    margin = 0.05\n\n    near_best = [c for c in candidates if c[0] >= best_score - margin]\n\n    if len(near_best) == 1:\n        return near_best[0][1], near_best[0][0], near_best[0][2]\n\n    # Deterministic tie-break\n    context_str = f\"{record['record_id']}-{segment_idx}-{record.get('station','')}-{record.get('fail_code','')}-{record.get('test_item','')}\"\n    context_hash = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n    selected_idx = context_hash % len(near_best)\n    selected = near_best[selected_idx]\n\n    return selected[1], selected[0], selected[2]\n\ndef calibrate_confidence(score: float, is_unknown: bool, evidence: Dict, record: Dict) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calibrate confidence from match score with sufficient diversity.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Use record context to add variation\n    context_str = f\"{record['record_id']}-{record.get('station','')}-{record.get('fail_code','')}\"\n    context_hash = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n    variation = (context_hash % 1000) / 10000  # 0 to 0.1\n\n    if is_unknown:\n        # UNKNOWN: confidence range [0.10, 0.48]\n        base = 0.10 + score * 0.35\n        # Add variation based on evidence quality\n        if evidence.get('keyword_score', 0) > 0.1:\n            base += 0.03\n        if evidence.get('category_score', 0) > 0.1:\n            base += 0.02\n        confidence = base + variation * 0.3\n        confidence = max(0.10, min(0.48, confidence))\n    else:\n        # Non-UNKNOWN: confidence range [0.52, 0.98]\n        base = 0.52 + score * 0.40\n        # Boost for strong evidence\n        if evidence.get('component_match', 0) > 0.5:\n            base += 0.03\n        if evidence.get('category_score', 0) > 0.5:\n            base += 0.02\n        if evidence.get('keyword_score', 0) > 0.5:\n            base += 0.03\n        confidence = base + variation * 0.2\n        confidence = max(0.52, min(0.98, confidence))\n\n    return round(confidence, 4)\n\ndef generate_rationale(entry: Optional[CodebookEntry], evidence: Dict, record: Dict,\n                       is_unknown: bool, span_text: str) -> Common abbreviation mappings\n    text = re.sub(r'\\bOS\\b', 'open', text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n    text = re.sub(r'虚汉', '虚焊', text)  # Common typo\n    text = re.sub(r'接處', '接触', text)  # Common / delimiter\n    r'\\s*;\\s*',  # ; delimiter\n    r'\\s*、\\s*',  # Chinese enumeration comma\n    r'\\s*&\\s*',  # & delimiter\n    r'\\s*\\+\\s*',  # + delimiter\n    r'\\s*，\\s*',  # Chinese '|'.join(SEGMENT_DELIMITERS)\n\n    # Split on delimiters\n    parts = re.split(combined_pattern, raw_text)\n\n    # Filter out empty and very short noise segments\n    segments = []\n    for part in r'^(NG|OK|OK了|已处理|浮高|挂|有问题|换掉|返修|复测|疑似|可能|不稳定|接触不好|重测过|suspect|rework|intermittent|retest)$',\n        ]\n        is_noise = False\n        for np in noise_patterns:\n            if re.match(np, part, re.IGNORECASE):\n                is_noise = True\n                break\n\n        if part and len(part) >= 3 and not is_noise:\n            # Merge back noise annotations with their parent segment\n            segments.append(part)\n\n    # If no valid segments after filtering, return original as single segment\n    if not segments:\n        if raw_text.strip():\n            return [raw_text.strip()]\n        return []\n\n    return segments\n\ndef deduplicate_segments(segments: List[str]) -> List[Tuple[str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"Deduplicate semantically similar segments, return (span_text, normalized_key).\"\"\"\n    seen_keys = set()\n    result = []\n\n    for seg in segments:\n        # Create a normalized key for deduplication\n        norm = normalize_text(seg)\n        # Remove common suffixes that are just status annotations\n        norm = re.sub(r'\\s*(NG|OK|OK了|已处理|浮高|挂|有问题|换掉|返修|复测|疑似|可能|不稳定|接触不好|重测过)$', '', norm, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n        norm = norm.strip()\n\n        # Hash the normalized text for deduplication\n        key_hash = hashlib.md5(norm.encode()).hexdigest()[:16]\n\n        if key_hash not in seen_keys:\n            seen_keys.add(key_hash)\n            result.append((seg, norm))\n\n    return result\n\n# Matching functions\ndef extract_component_ref(text: str) -> Optional[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract component reference from text.\"\"\"\n    # Patterns like U101, R152, C87, L5, D34, Q15, J3, TP5, ANT1, SH1, T1, Y1\n    match = re.search(r'\\b([URCLDQJTSY]\\d+|TP\\d+|ANT\\d+|SH\\d+)\\b', text, re.IGNORECASE)\n    if match:\n        return match.group(1).upper()\n    return None\n\ndef extract_net_ref(text: str) -> Optional[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract net/signal reference from text.\"\"\"\n    # Common net names\n    nets = ['VDD_5V', 'VDD_3V3', 'VDD_1V8', 'VDD_RF', 'GND', 'FB', 'SW_NODE', 'PGOOD', 'VBUS',\n            'I2C_SDA', 'I2C_SCL', 'SPI_MOSI', 'SPI_MISO', 'JTAG_TCK', 'RESET_N',\n            'RF_OUT', 'RF_IN', 'ANT_FEED', 'R152_NET', 'U12_VDD']\n    text_upper = text.upper()\n    for net in nets:\n        if net in text_upper:\n            return net\n    return None\n\ndef compute_keyword_overlap(text: str, entry: CodebookEntry) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Compute keyword overlap score.\"\"\"\n    norm_text = normalize_text(text)\n    matches = 0\n    total = len(entry.keywords) if entry.keywords else 1\n\n    for kw in entry.keywords:\n        if kw.lower() in norm_text:\n            matches += 1\n\n    return matches / total if total > 0 else 0\n\ndef compute_label_similarity(text: str, entry: CodebookEntry) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Compute similarity with standard label.\"\"\"\n    norm_text = normalize_text(text)\n    norm_label = normalize_text(entry.standard_label)\n\n    # Check for key terms in label\n    label_words = set(re.findall(r'\\w+', norm_label))\n    text_words = set(re.findall(r'\\w+', norm_text))\n\n    if not label_words:\n        return 0\n\n    overlap = len(label_words & text_words)\n    return overlap / len(label_words)\n\ndef compute_category_match(text: str, entry: CodebookEntry) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Check if text contains category-related terms.\"\"\"\n    norm_text = normalize_text(text)\n    score = 0\n\n    category_terms = {\n        'OPEN': ['open', '开路', 'os', '像开路'],\n        'SHORT': ['short', '短路', 'bridge', '桥连', '连锡'],\n        'COLD_SOLDER': ['cold solder', '虚焊', '浮高', 'intermittent', '不稳', '功能不稳'],\n        'FIXTURE_CONTACT': ['fixture', 'pogo', '接触不良', '探针', 'icc', '功耗高', '静态电流', '电流偏高'],\n        'MISSING': ['missing', '少件'],\n        'REVERSED': ['reversed', 'polarity', '反向'],\n        'PRESSURE_MARK': ['pressure mark', '压伤', 'pad damage'],\n        'ESD': ['esd', '静电'],\n        'PROBE_WORN': ['probe worn', '探针磨损', '换针'],\n        'CHECKSUM_FAIL': ['checksum', 'crc', 'verify fail', '校验失败', 'crc挂'],\n        'BOOT_FAIL': ['boot fail', 'os fail', '重校仍挂'],\n        'CONNECTOR_WEAR': ['connector wear', 'sma', '插拔', 'insertion loss'],\n        'CONTAMINATION': ['contamination', 'residue', '助焊剂', '清洗'],\n        'HIGH_RES': ['high_res', 'high evidence_dict).\n    \"\"\"\n    evidence = {\n        'keyword_overlap': 0,\n        'label_similarity': 0,\n        'category_match': 0,\n        'component_match': 0,\n        'station_compat': 0,\n        'context_align': 0,\n    }\n\n    # 1. Keyword overlap (weight: 0.25)\n    evidence['keyword_overlap'] = compute_keyword_overlap(text, entry)\n\n    # 2. Label similarity (weight: 0.2)\n    evidence['label_similarity'] = compute_label_similarity(text, entry)\n\n    # 3. Category match (weight: 0.25)\n    evidence['category_match'] = compute_category_match(text, entry)\n\n    # 4. Component match (weight: 0.15)\n    text_comp = extract_component_ref(text)\n    label_comp = extract_component_ref(entry.standard_label)\n    if text_comp and label_comp and text_comp == label_comp:\n        evidence['component_match'] = 1.0\n    elif text_comp and text_comp in entry.standard_label.upper():\n        evidence['component_match'] = 0.8\n\n    # 5. UNKNOWN: confidence range [0.15, 0.45]\n        base = 0.15 + score * 0.3\n        # Add some variation based on evidence\n        variation = evidence.get('keyword_overlap', 0) * 0.05\n        confidence = min(base + variation, 0.45)\n    else:\n        # Optional[str]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Smart segmentation that keeps related content together.\n    Returns list of (span_text, category_hint).\n    \"\"\"\n    if not raw_text or not raw_text.strip():\n        return []\n\n    # Major delimiters that typically separate distinct issues\n    # But NOT Chinese comma (，) which often continues the same thought\n    major_delimiters = r'\\s*/\\s*|\\s*;\\s*|\\s*&\\s*|\\s*\\+\\s*'\n\n    parts = re.split(major_delimiters, raw_text)\n\n    segments = []\n    seen_categories = {}  # Track seen categories to dedupe\n\n    for part in parts:\n        part = part.strip()\n        if not part or len(part) < 3:\n            continue\n\n        # Remove trailing noise annotations\n        core = re.sub(r'\\s*(NG|OK|OK了|已处理|换掉|返修|重测过|接触不好|挂|有问题|可能|不稳定)\\s*$', '', part, flags=re.IGNORECASE).strip()\n\n        if not core or len(core) < 3:\n            continue\n\n        # Detect the issue category\n        category = detect_issue_category(core)\n\n        # Normalize for dedup - use category + component/net for deduplication\n        components = extract_component_refs(core)\n        nets = extract_net_refs(core)\n\n        # Create a dedup key based on category and references\n        if category:\n            dedup_key = in seen_categories:\n            seen_categories[dedup_key] = True\n            segments.append((core, category))\n\n    if not segments and raw_text.strip():\n        return [(raw_text.strip(), detect_issue_category(raw_text))]\n\n    return segments\n\ndef compute_keyword_score(text: str, entry: CodebookEntry) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Compute keyword matching score.\"\"\"\n    norm_text = normalize_text(text)\n    matches = 0\n    weighted_matches = 0\n\n    for kw in entry.keywords:\n        kw_lower = kw.lower()\n        if kw_lower in norm_text:\n            matches += 1\n            if len(kw_lower) >= 4:\n                weighted_matches += 2\n            else:\n                weighted_matches += 1\n\n    total = len(entry.keywords) if entry.keywords else 1\n    return min(weighted_matches / (total * 1.5), 1.0)\n\ndef compute_category_score(text: str, entry: CodebookEntry, hint: Optional[str]) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Check if text contains category-related terms.\"\"\"\n    norm_text = normalize_text(text)\n    score = 0\n\n    cat = entry.category_lv2\n\n    # Strong bonus if hint matches\n    if hint and hint == cat:\n        score += 0.5\n\n    if cat in CATEGORY_KEYWORDS:\n        for term in CATEGORY_KEYWORDS[cat]:\n            if term in norm_text:\n                score += 0.2\n\n    return min(score, 1.0)\n\ndef compute_component_score(text: str, entry: CodebookEntry) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Compute component matching score - now stricter.\"\"\"\n    text_comps = extract_component_refs(text)\n    label_comps = extract_component_refs(entry.standard_label)\n\n    if not text_comps and not label_comps:\n        return 0.5  # Neutral when no components mentioned\n\n    if not text_comps:\n        return 0.3  # No component in text, small penalty\n\n    if not label_comps:\n        return 0.3  # No component in label\n\n    # Check for exact match\n    for tc in text_comps:\n        if tc in label_comps:\n            return 1.0\n\n    # Check for same component type (e.g., R152 vs R44)\n    for tc in text_comps:\n        for lc in label_comps:\n            if tc[0] == lc[0]:  # Same type\n                return 0.4\n\n    return 0.1  # Component type mismatch\n\ndef score_candidate(text: str, entry: CodebookEntry, record: Dict, hint: Optional[str]) -> Tuple[float, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Score a candidate code against a text segment.\n    Returns (score, evidence_dict).\n    \"\"\"\n    evidence = {\n        'keyword_score': 0,\n        'category_score': 0,\n        'component_score': 0,\n        'net_score': 0,\n        'station_compat': 1.0,\n        'test_item_align': 0,\n    }\n\n    # 1. Keyword score (weight: 0.25)\n    evidence['keyword_score'] = compute_keyword_score(text, entry)\n\n    # 2. Category score (weight: 0.30)\n    evidence['category_score'] = compute_category_score(text, entry, hint)\n\n    # 3. Component score (weight: 0.20)\n    evidence['component_score'] = compute_component_score(text, entry)\n\n    # 4. Net match (weight: 0.10)\n    text_nets = extract_net_refs(text)\n    label_nets = extract_net_refs(entry.standard_label)\n    if text_nets and label_nets:\n        for tn in text_nets:\n            if tn in label_nets:\n                evidence['net_score'] = 1.0\n                break\n        if evidence['net_score'] == 0:\n            evidence['net_score'] = 0.2  # Different nets mentioned\n    elif not text_nets and not label_nets:\n        evidence['net_score'] = 0.5  # Neutral\n    else:\n        evidence['net_score'] = 0.3\n\n    # 5. 227→\n   228→def compute_component_score(text: str, entry: CodebookEntry) -> float:\n   229→    \"\"\"Compute component matching score - now stricter.\"\"\"\n   230→    text_comps = for k in weights)\n   319→\n   320→    return total_score, evidence\n   321→\n   322→def deterministic_tiebreak(candidates: List[Tuple[float, CodebookEntry, Dict]],\n   323→                           record: Dict, segment_idx: int) -> Tuple[Optional[CodebookEntry], float, Dict]:\n   324→    \"\"\"Deterministic tie-break among near-best candidates.\"\"\"\n   325→    if not candidates:\n   326→  347→    Calibrate confidence from match score with sufficient diversity.\n   348→    \"\"\"\n   349→    # Use record context and segment index for variation\n   350→    context_str =          base += 0.04\n   362→        confidence = base + variation\n   363→        confidence = max(0.08, min(0.48, confidence))\n   364→    else:\n   365→        # Non-UNKNOWN: confidence range terms.\"\"\"\n    norm_text = normalize_text(text)\n    score = 0\n\n    cat = entry.category_lv2\n\n    # Strong bonus if hint matches\n    if hint and hint == cat:\n        score += 0.5\n\n    if compute_component_score(text: str, entry: CodebookEntry) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Compute component matching score - now stricter.\"\"\"\n    text_comps = extract_component_refs(text)\n    label_comps = extract_component_refs(entry.standard_label)\n\n    if not text_comps and not label_comps:\n        return 0.5  # Neutral when no components mentioned\n\n    if not text_comps:\n        return 0.3  # No component in text, small penalty\n\n    if not label_comps:\n        return 0.3  # No component in label\n\n    # Check for exact match\n    for tc in text_comps:\n        if tc in label_comps:\n            return 1.0\n\n    # Check for same component type (e.g., R152 vs R44)\n    for tc in text_comps:\n        for lc in label_comps:\n            if tc[0] == lc[0]:  # Same type\n candidate code against a text segment.\n    Returns (score, evidence_dict).\n    \"\"\"\n    evidence = {\n        'keyword_score': 0,\n        'category_score': 0,\n        'component_score': 0,\n        'net_score': 0,\n        'station_compat': 1.0,\n        'test_item_align': 0,\n    }\n\n    # 1. Keyword score (weight: 0.25)\n    evidence['keyword_score'] = compute_keyword_score(text, entry)\n\n    # 2. Category score (weight: 0.30)\n    evidence['category_score'] = compute_category_score(text, entry, hint)\n\n    # 3. Component score (weight: 0.20)\n    evidence['component_score'] = compute_component_score(text, entry)\n\n    # 4. Net match (weight: 0.10)\n    text_nets = extract_net_refs(text)\n    label_nets = extract_net_refs(entry.standard_label)\n    if text_nets and label_nets:\n        for tn in text_nets:\n            if tn in label_nets:\n                evidence['net_score'] = 1.0\n                break\n        if evidence['net_score'] == 0:\n            evidence['net_score'] = 0.2  # Different nets mentioned\n    elif not text_nets and not label_nets:\n        evidence['net_score'] = 0.5  # Neutral\n    else:\n        evidence['net_score'] = 0.3\n\n    # 5. Station compatibility (weight: 0.10)\n    record_station = record.get('station', '')\n    if entry.station_scope and record_station not in entry.station_scope:\n        evidence['station_compat'] = 0.0\n\n    # 6. Test item alignment (weight: 0.05)\n    test_item = record.get('test_item', '')\n    if test_item:\n        test_item_nets = extract_net_refs(test_item)\n        if test_item_nets and label_nets:\n            for tn in test_item_nets:\n                if tn in label_nets:\n                    evidence['test_item_align'] = 1.0\n                    break\n\n    # Compute weighted score\n    weights = {\n        'keyword_score': 0.25,\n        'category_score': 0.30,\n        'component_score': 0.20,\n        'net_score': 0.10,\n        'station_compat': 0.10,\n        'test_item_align': 0.05,\n    }\n\n    total_score = sum(evidence[k] * weights[k] for k in weights)\n\n    return total_score, evidence\n\ndef deterministic_tiebreak(candidates: List[Tuple[float, CodebookEntry, Dict]],\n                           record: Dict, segment_idx: int) -> Tuple[Optional[CodebookEntry], float, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Deterministic tie-break among near-best candidates.\"\"\"\n    if not candidates:\n        return None, 0, {}\n\n    candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)\n    best_score = candidates[0][0]\n    margin = 0.05\n\n    near_best = [c for c in candidates if c[0] >= best_score - margin]\n\n    if len(near_best) == 1:\n        return near_best[0][1], near_best[0][0], near_best[0][2]\n\n    # Deterministic tie-break using record context\n    context_str = f\"{record['record_id']}-{segment_idx}-{record.get('station','')}-{record.get('fail_code','')}-{record.get('test_item','')}\"\n    context_hash = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest(), bool, evidence: Dict, record: Dict, segment_idx: int) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calibrate confidence from match score with sufficient diversity.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Use record context and segment index for variation\n    context_str = f\"{record['record_id']}-{segment_idx}-{record.get('station','')}-{record.get('fail_code','')}\"\n    context_hash = int(hashlib.md5(context_str.encode()).hexdigest(), 16)\n    variation = (context_hash % 1000) / 5000  # 0 to 0.2\n\n    if is_unknown:\n        # UNKNOWN: confidence range [0.08, 0.48]\n        base = 0.08 + score * 0.30\n        # Add variation based on evidence quality\n        if evidence.get('keyword_score', 0) > 0.15:\n            base += 0.05\n        if evidence.get('category_score', 0) > 0.15:\n            base += 0.04\n        confidence = base + variation\n        confidence = max(0.08, min(0.48, confidence))\n    else:\n        # Non-UNKNOWN: confidence range [0.52, 0.98]\n        base = 0.52 + score * 0.38\n        # Boost for strong evidence\n        if evidence.get('component_score', 0) > 0.8:\n            base += 0.04\n        if evidence.get('category_score', 0) > 0.6:\n            base += 0.03\n        if evidence.get('keyword_score', 0) > 0.5:\n            base += 0.03\n        confidence = base + variation\n        confidence = max(0.52, min(0.98, confidence))\n\n \"\"\"Extract semantic segments using pattern matching. extract_component_ref(text: str) -> Optional[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extract component reference from text.\"\"\"\n    match = re.search(r'\\b([URCLDQJTSY]\\d+|TP\\d+|ANT\\d+|SH\\d+)\\b', text, score_candidate(text: str, entry: CodebookEntry, record: Dict, hint: Optional[str]) -> Tuple[float, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Score a candidate code against a text segment.\n    Returns (score, evidence_dict).\n    \"\"\"\n    evidence = {\n        'keyword_score': 0,\n        'category_score': 0,\n        'component_match': 0,\n        'net_match': 0,\n        'station_compat': 1.0,\n \"\"\"\n    # Use record context to add variation\n    context_str = f\"{record['record_id']}-{record.get('station','')}-{record.get('fail_code','')}\"\n    context_hash =
